мэдди
жмот
кальпиди
туба
пичугина
зубатов
виргиния
]=]

local words = {}
for word in rawWords:gmatch("[^\r\n]+") do
    table.insert(words, word)
end

local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
gui.Name = "WordHelperGui"
gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
gui.DisplayOrder = 50
gui.Parent = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

local main = Instance.new("Frame")
main.Name = "Main"
main.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(360, 450)
main.Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 70)
main.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 27, 35)
main.BorderSizePixel = 0
main.Parent = gui
Instance.new("UICorner", main).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(75, 80, 100)
stroke.Thickness = 1
stroke.Parent = main

local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 32)
title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 4)
title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
title.Text = "Словарь — " .. #words .. " слов"
title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255)
title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
title.TextSize = 15
title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
title.Parent = main

local function makeLabel(parent, name, text, pos, size)
    local obj = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    obj.Name = name
    obj.Position = pos
    obj.Size = size
    obj.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    obj.Text = text
    obj.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 238, 250)
    obj.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    obj.TextSize = 13
    obj.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    obj.Parent = parent
    return obj
end

local function makeBox(parent, name, placeholder, position, size)
    local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
    box.Name = name
    box.Position = position
    box.Size = size
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(39, 42, 53)
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.PlaceholderText = placeholder
    box.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 165, 180)
    box.Text = ""
    box.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(250, 250, 255)
    box.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    box.TextSize = 14
    box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    box.Parent = parent
    Instance.new("UICorner", box).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)
    local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
    padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 9)
    padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 7)
    padding.Parent = box
    return box
end

local function makeButton(parent, text, position, size, color)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Position = position
    b.Size = size
    b.BackgroundColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(57, 67, 96)
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.Text = text
    b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(250, 250, 255)
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    b.TextSize = 13
    b.Parent = parent
    Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)
    return b
end

-- Вкладки
local tab1 = makeButton(main, "Словарь", UDim2.fromOffset(12, 40), UDim2.new(0.5, -18, 0, 30), Color3.fromRGB(70, 85, 125))
local tab2 = makeButton(main, "Рандомное слово", UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 0, 40), UDim2.new(0.5, -18, 0, 30), Color3.fromRGB(43, 46, 58))

local dictionaryPage = Instance.new("Frame")
dictionaryPage.Name = "DictionaryPage"
dictionaryPage.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 76)

    dictionaryPage.Visible = true
    randomPage.Visible = false
    tab1.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 85, 125)
    tab2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(43, 46, 58)
end)

    dictionaryPage.Visible = false
    randomPage.Visible = true
    tab2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 85, 125)
    tab1.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(43, 46, 58)
end)

-- Вкладка 1: все совпадения доступны страницами, чтобы тысячи слов не подвешивали GUI.
local letterBox = makeBox(dictionaryPage, "RequiredLetter", "Введи первую букву", UDim2.fromOffset(12, 8), UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 32))
local searchBox = makeBox(dictionaryPage, "Search", "Дополнительный поиск", UDim2.fromOffset(12, 46), UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 32))
local countLabel = makeLabel(dictionaryPage, "Count", "Введи букву, чтобы увидеть варианты", UDim2.fromOffset(13, 81), UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 20))
local list = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
list.Name = "WordList"
list.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 104)
list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, -180)
list.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(19, 21, 28)
list.BorderSizePixel = 0
list.ScrollBarThickness = 5
list.CanvasSize = UDim2.new()
list.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
list.Parent = dictionaryPage
Instance.new("UICorner", list).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 7)
local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)
layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
layout.Parent = list
local listPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
listPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 5)
listPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 5)
listPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 5)
listPadding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 5)
listPadding.Parent = list
local output = makeBox(dictionaryPage, "SelectedWord", "Выбранное слово появится здесь", UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -68), UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 30))
output.TextEditable = false
local prevButton = makeButton(dictionaryPage, "← Назад", UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -34), UDim2.new(0.5, -18, 0, 28))
local nextButton = makeButton(dictionaryPage, "Далее →", UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 1, -34), UDim2.new(0.5, -18, 0, 28))
local PAGE_SIZE = 150
local filtered = {}
local page = 1
local renderToken = 0

local function refresh()
    renderToken += 1
    local token = renderToken
    filtered = {}
    local required = string.lower(letterBox.Text):gsub("%s+", "")
    local query = string.lower(searchBox.Text):gsub("%s+", "")
    for _, word in ipairs(words) do
        local lower = string.lower(word)
        local matchesLetter = required == "" or string.sub(lower, 1, #required) == required
        local matchesSearch = query == "" or string.find(lower, query, 1, true) ~= nil
        if matchesLetter and matchesSearch then table.insert(filtered, word) end
    end
    page = math.clamp(page, 1, math.max(1, math.ceil(#filtered / PAGE_SIZE)))
    countLabel.Text = string.format("Найдено: %d | Страница %d из %d", #filtered, page, math.max(1, math.ceil(#filtered / PAGE_SIZE)))
    for _, child in ipairs(list:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("TextButton") or child.Name == "EmptyMessage" then child:Destroy() end
    end
    local first = (page - 1) * PAGE_SIZE + 1
    local last = math.min(page * PAGE_SIZE, #filtered)
    for index = first, last do
        if token ~= renderToken then return end
        local word = filtered[index]
        local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
        button.Name = "Word_" .. index
        button.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0, 27)
        button.BackgroundColor3 = index % 2 == 0 and Color3.fromRGB(35, 38, 48) or Color3.fromRGB(43, 46, 58)
        button.BorderSizePixel = 0
        button.Text = word
        button.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 238, 250)
        button.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        button.TextSize = 14
        button.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        button.LayoutOrder = index
        button.Parent = list
        Instance.new("UICorner", button).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
        pad.Parent = button
        button.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() output.Text = word end)
    end
    if #filtered == 0 then
        local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        empty.Name = "EmptyMessage"
        empty.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 32)
        empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        empty.Text = "Ничего не найдено"
        empty.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 175, 190)
        empty.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        empty.TextSize = 13
        empty.Parent = list
    end
    prevButton.Active = page > 1
    prevButton.AutoButtonColor = page > 1
    nextButton.Active = page < math.max(1, math.ceil(#filtered / PAGE_SIZE))
    nextButton.AutoButtonColor = nextButton.Active
end

letterBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() page = 1; refresh() end)
searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() page = 1; refresh() end)
prevButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if page > 1 then page -= 1; refresh() end end)
nextButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() if page < math.ceil(#filtered / PAGE_SIZE) then page += 1; refresh() end end)
refresh()

-- Вкладка 2: случайное слово на указанную букву.
local randomLetterBox = makeBox(randomPage, "RandomLetter", "Введи букву", UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12), UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 36))
local randomizeButton = makeButton(randomPage, "ЗАРАНДОМИТЬ", UDim2.fromOffset(12, 56), UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 38), Color3.fromRGB(65, 105, 85))
local randomResult = Instance.new("TextLabel")
randomResult.Name = "RandomResult"
randomResult.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 108)
randomResult.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 70)
randomResult.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(19, 21, 28)
randomResult.BorderSizePixel = 0
randomResult.Text = "Случайное слово появится здесь"
randomResult.TextWrapped = true
randomResult.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255)
randomResult.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
randomResult.TextSize = 22
randomResult.Parent = randomPage
Instance.new("UICorner", randomResult).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
local copyRandom = makeButton(randomPage, "Выбрать это слово", UDim2.fromOffset(12, 188), UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 34))
local randomStatus = makeLabel(randomPage, "Status", "", UDim2.fromOffset(13, 228), UDim2.new(1, -26, 0, 40))
local lastRandomWord = ""
local function chooseRandom()
    local required = string.lower(randomLetterBox.Text):gsub("%s+", "")
    if required == "" then
        randomStatus.Text = "Сначала введи букву."
        randomResult.Text = "Введите букву"
        lastRandomWord = ""
        return
    end
    local candidates = {}
    for _, word in ipairs(words) do
        if string.sub(string.lower(word), 1, #required) == required then
            table.insert(candidates, word)
        end
    end
    if #candidates == 0 then
        randomStatus.Text = "Слов на эту букву не найдено."
        randomResult.Text = "Нет вариантов"
        lastRandomWord = ""
        return
    end
    lastRandomWord = candidates[math.random(1, #candidates)]
    randomResult.Text = lastRandomWord
    randomStatus.Text = "Вариантов на эту букву: " .. #candidates
end
randomizeButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(chooseRandom)
copyRandom.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if lastRandomWord ~= "" then
        output.Text = lastRandomWord
        randomStatus.Text = "Слово выбрано: " .. lastRandomWord .. " (оно доступно во вкладке «Словарь» тоже)"
    else
        randomStatus.Text = "Сначала нажми «ЗАРАНДОМИТЬ»."
    end
end)

-- Right Shift открывает/закрывает меню.
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
    if processed then return end
    if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.RightShift then gui.Enabled = not gui.Enabled end
end)

-- Перетаскивание панели за верхнюю часть.
local dragging, dragStart, startPos
main.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
    if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
        dragging = true
        dragStart = input.Position
        startPos = main.Position
        input.Changed:Connect(function()
            if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
        end)
    end
end)
UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
    if dragging and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        main.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
    end
end)
